ParlaMint-PL_2022-05-11-sejm-54-1.u676	Statement on May 11th Day without Garbage In my statement, I would like to draw attention to a relatively unusual but very important holiday celebrated annually on 11 May, called World Day without Garbage. The initiative was born on the basis of an idea developed by young people from several countries cooperating in the framework of the European Youth Parliament's international programme in 2007, a project by Pro Europe - an association of organisations operating under the Green Point recovery organisations. The purpose of this grand day, whose intentions we should read and reflect every day, is to promote correct rules for the segregation of waste, to promote ecological attitudes and to protect the environment. It also prepares educational programs and materials, implementing them both in the real sphere - in the field, as well as in the digital reality - on-line. Everything is accompanied by educational events throughout Poland. The promoters of this special day do not produce thousands of posters, leaflets, inserts and stickers. The basic educational material of each edition of the action is an electronic card, which, when clicked, redirects to the website. A day without rubbish. en. I would like, through my statement, to encourage you to join this event not only since Christmas, but also on a daily basis.
ParlaMint-PL_2022-05-11-sejm-54-1.u677	When I made a statement a few months ago about the end of the September campaign, I forgot one thing. I escaped that by the end of April 1940 it was officially operating, not in the underground, the last branch of the army of the Second Republic, i.e. the Separated Branch of the Polish Army under the command of Major. Henryk Dobrzanski ¿Hubalaż. Dobrzanski was an insubordinate soul. This perfect soldier, decorated with the Order of Virtuti Militari, was able to tell his superiors what he thought of them - not always in choosy words. That's why he spent most of his peaceful life in subconscious garrisons. Some researchers even claim that he was not expelled simply because his close relative was a good friend of President Ignacy Mościcki. After the outbreak of the war in September 1939, as deputy commander of the 110th Reservoir of the Ulanów, he did not comply with the order to dissolve the unit and decided to go to the aid of the fighting Warsaw. When she fell, he decided to stay in the country and continue his military struggle in uniform. The Armed Forces were doing everything they could to get a small branch of Dobrzański, who accepted the pseudonym Hubal, to solve it. This decision was based on the desire to protect the civilian population supporting the Hubals from the German repression. Meanwhile, Major Dobrzański organized his small army, believing that he was creating a new formation, which in the future will be a staff for the re-created units. He trained recruits, his soldiers formed security patrols and maintained order in the places where they were stationed. The legend passed through the participation of the Hubals in the Holy Mass at Christmas on Christmas 1939, when the branch He entered the church in full uniform. While fleeing the German raids, Hubal fought several regular battles, the largest of which took place at the Hutch on 30 March 1940. Unfortunately, the population in the villages where the Hubals were stationed was punished by the Germans with further pacifications during which the Germans murdered. Dobrzanski lived through this very hard. He blamed himself for what happened. When most soldiers withdrew from him, he tried to break through the German cordon with the others. On April 30, 1940, he was killed by a German bullet. The anniversary of his death took place a few days ago. Hubal's character is still controversial. Some judge him of honor and faith and blame him for the death of civilians. I guess he just didn't understand that this new war wasn't like the last one. This new war was an idea war. The Germans wanted not only to win, but also simply to destroy the Polish people and other nations. Hubal thought like a soldier and remained a soldier to the end. He said I'm not gonna take off my uniform, and I'm not gonna put my gun down.
ParlaMint-PL_2022-05-11-sejm-54-1.u678	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On 4 May we celebrated the festival of firefighters in our country, whose patron saint is St. Florian, but also the chimney guy's day. Both of these professions require courage, knowledge and sacrifice. We are well aware that the help of firefighters in various situations is invaluable, not only for professional ones serving in the State Fire Department, but also for those of volunteer firefighters who actively operate in rural areas in practically every municipality. It is good that we remember them not only on the occasion of their holiday, when we wish and thank you. I myself had the opportunity to do so by participating in the District Firefighter's Day Obats in Szczucin in the district of Dąbrowski. It is extremely important that the firefighters receive professional equipment, that modern fire and fire trucks are systematically transferred to their disposal. They receive increasing support from the government, but it is also worth emphasising the attitude of local governments that care and care about firefighters throughout the year. I would also like to express my special gratitude to all those who have contributed to the fact that voluntary firefighters will be able to receive a special cash benefit for retirement. It is also worth thanking those who risk their time for us over the years. Not everyone remembers, however, that the fourth of May is also a chimney sweep day. Contrary to some popular opinions, chimneying is not an easy and responsible profession. I could see the high sense of responsibility these people must have on a daily basis by participating in the National Congress of Cominaries in Ryglice in Tarnów County. Thanks to their accuracy and accuracy, many threats to human life and health, such as fires or clogging, are avoided. They contribute to protecting the environment and reducing the consumption of fuels used to heat buildings. The smooth chimneys are clean air. Therefore, it is worth appreciating them and wishing the chimney sweeps a lot of happiness and joy in their work, so needed for our society.
